Result: 在Tmax晋升划分上，CoTrace使Qwen3.5-9B在SFT下从78个任务提升至88个，在线RL变体达90个；紧凑的harness匹配语料比跨兄弟harness的大规模混合语料更高效；Terminal-Bench 2.1和SWE-bench Lite显示OOD迁移取决于harness兼容性，训练与评估运行时一致可避免程序性执行崩溃。

Conclusion: 轨迹数据需按其生成harness进行匹配与路由，harness兼容性是终端agent能力协同演化与跨分布迁移的关键，紧凑匹配语料优于大规模混合语料。

Abstract: Terminal-agent capability depends jointly on model weights and the runtime harness that formats prompts, binds tools, and handles error recovery. Existing harness-model co-evolution approaches improve both components, yet often treat trajectories produced during harness search as an undifferentiated replay buffer. This practice overlooks that a trajectory's value for model training depends on the harness under which it was generated. To systematically analyze this interface, we establish an alternating co-evolution framework that decouples harness search and policy training through component-wise promotion decisions. Within this framework, we introduce CoTrace, a harness-aware data recipe that explicitly governs trajectory routing, provenance matching, and curriculum refresh. Under CoTrace, recurring execution failures guide harness synthesis, while policy training is strictly conditioned on verified rollouts matched to the adopted runtime for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) or fresh online interactions for reinforcement learning (RL). On the Tmax promotion split, CoTrace advances Qwen3.5-9B from 78 to 88 solved tasks under supervised fine-tuning while an online reinforcement variant reaches 90. Specifically, a compact harness-matched corpus produces steady model gains at substantially lower compute than much larger corpora pooled across sibling harnesses. Furthermore, evaluations on Terminal-Bench 2.1 and SWE-bench Lite show that out-of-distribution transfer depends fundamentally on harness compatibility, where maintaining consistency between training and evaluation runtimes prevents procedural execution breakdowns observed under foreign scaffolds.

</details>


### [218] [PHRBench: A Behavioral Evaluation of Post-Hallucination Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10455)
*Linghao Meng,Feng He,Xuan Yang,Junyuan Mao,Pinze Ren,Deqing Mu,Hesen Yang,Qiankun Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PHRBench基准，从行为层面刻画LLM在多阶段系统中处理幻觉前提的推理过程，发现成功纠错罕见且与信念更新频率相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对后幻觉推理（PHR）的研究主要关注最终结果变化和聚合层面的推理动态，未能在响应层面充分理解模型如何解决幻觉前提。

Method: 构建PHRBench受控基准，覆盖4个领域和18个LLM；独立于最终答案正确性地刻画每条推理轨迹，划分为幻觉顺从、幻觉回避和启发式纠正三类，并将"成功纠正且最终达到正确答案"定义为有洞察力的轨迹。

Result: 在4820个受控实例上，成功恢复较罕见，且与推理轨迹中更频繁的信念更新相关；幻觉提示的特征对成功恢复具有显著预测力，轻量级预测器AUROC达0.847。

Conclusion: 提供后幻觉推理的行为视角，刻画LLM如何解决错误上下文以及成功恢复何时可能发生。

Abstract: Hallucinated information can propagate through multi-stage LLM systems and become part of the context for subsequent reasoning. Existing studies of post-hallucination reasoning (PHR) mainly characterize changes in final outcomes and aggregate reasoning dynamics, leaving how models resolve hallucinated premises at the response level insufficiently understood. In this work, we introduce PHRBench, a controlled benchmark for behaviorally structured PHR across four domains and 18 large language models. PHRBench characterizes each reasoning trajectory independently of final-answer correctness through Hallucination Compliance, Hallucination Avoidance, and Heuristic Correction, and defines an insightful trajectory as successful correction that ultimately reaches the correct answer. Across 4820 controlled instances, we find that successful recovery remains relatively rare and is associated with more frequent belief updates along the reasoning trajectory. We further find that properties of the hallucinated prompt contain substantial predictive signal for successful recovery, with a lightweight predictor achieving an AUROC of 0.847. These findings provide a behavioral view of post-hallucination reasoning, characterizing how LLMs resolve erroneous context and when successful recovery is likely to occur.

</details>


### [219] [Your Prompt Should Do More: Effects of Retrieval Instructions in Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10508)
*Amanda Myntti,Jenna Kanerva,Veronika Laippala,Filip Ginter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究检索指令如何影响非对称检索中的查询表示，发现查询端干扰项会导致模型无法遵循指令，通过加入干扰项微调可显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前提示式嵌入模型在检索任务中难以可靠遵循详细指令，现有研究缺乏对指令如何影响查询表示的机制分析。

Method: 分析指令对非对称检索查询表示的影响机制，并在评估中加入查询端干扰项；通过带干扰项的训练数据微调嵌入模型。

Result: 模型在存在查询端干扰项时即使简单任务指令也无法遵循；加入查询端干扰项微调后性能显著提升，且对其他任务影响极小。

Conclusion: 指令遵循失败与当前嵌入模型的训练设置及评估方式有关，引入查询端干扰项训练是一种有效改进方向。

Abstract: Prompted embedding models have recently received increasing attention, particularly for retrieval, where detailed retrieval instructions are provided as part of the retrieval prompt. Several new datasets and studies have examined this setting, showing that the current embedding models often struggle to follow such instructions reliably. In this paper, we study the mechanism of how instructions actually affect the representations of retrieval queries in asymmetric retrieval tasks. We show that models can fail to follow even simple task instructions when query-side distractors are included in the evaluation. We hypothesize that this behavior is driven by the training setup of current embedding models and their evaluation, and show that fine-tuning with added query-side distractors leads to substantial improvements, with minimal effect on other tasks.

</details>


### [220] [EngramEdit: Decoupled Knowledge Updates in LLMs through Conditional Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10533)
*Hongru Cai,Ran Wei,Wenjie Wang,Chengfa Wu,Ning Song,Yongqi Li,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EngramEdit，通过条件记忆实现解耦的知识更新，编辑成功率高且保留无关知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件记忆架构（如DeepSeek Engram）用n-gram查表扩展LLM容量，有望将事实知识存储与通用计算解耦，实现固定主干下的知识更新。但同一事实的不同表达会激活不同n-gram嵌入，且更新共享嵌入会误改其他事实预测。

Method: 先计算目标记忆表示，使模型在多种表达下预测更新后事实；再联合更新共享n-gram嵌入以匹配这些目标，并对高频复用嵌入施加更强惩罚以保护无关知识。

Result: 实现近乎完美的编辑成功率；新知识可泛化到未见表达和多跳推理，CoT提示下准确率约为最强基线的三倍；知识累积更新时无关知识与通用能力基本保持。

Conclusion: EngramEdit将条件记忆转化为可编辑的知识接口，使其作用超越模型扩容，支持解耦的知识更新。

Abstract: Conditional memory architectures such as DeepSeek Engram use input n-grams to look up learned embeddings, expanding the capacity of large language models (LLMs) with limited additional computation. Beyond model scaling, this architecture has demonstrated the potential to decouple factual knowledge storage from general-purpose computation, offering a promising route to updating factual knowledge while keeping the Transformer backbone fixed. Realizing this potential is challenging because different expressions of a fact may activate different n-gram embeddings, while updating shared embeddings can unintentionally change the model's predictions about other facts. We propose EngramEdit for decoupled knowledge updates through conditional memory. EngramEdit first computes target memory representations that make the model predict the updated fact across multiple expressions. It then jointly updates the shared n-gram embeddings to match these targets across expressions and edits, penalizing updates to frequently reused embeddings more strongly to preserve unrelated knowledge. Experiments show that EngramEdit enables independent factual knowledge updates through conditional memory, achieving near-perfect editing success. Revised knowledge is usable across unseen expressions and in multi-hop reasoning, with nearly three times the strongest baseline's accuracy under chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting. Unrelated knowledge and general capabilities are largely preserved even as factual updates accumulate. These findings show that EngramEdit turns conditional memory into an editable knowledge interface, extending its role beyond model scaling to support decoupled knowledge updates.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [FLoRa: Flight-Assisted Data Collection from Duty Cycling LoRa Nodes under Energy Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09226)
*Naresh Babu Kakarla,V. Mahendran*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: FLoRa提出了一种飞行辅助LoRa数据采集架构，结合SA、CMA-ES和POMDP解决UAV在能量受限下的路由、悬停与探测决策问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRa IoT设备为省电而占空比运行，导致UAV数据采集困难。UAV需在能量约束下决定访问哪些节点、顺序、悬停位置及探测次数，同时数据新鲜度随时间衰减，问题涉及多级优化与不确定性，难以求解。

Method: 采用多级优化架构：SA做路径规划，CMA-ES做悬停定位，POMDP做探测决策。提出VIP指标量化采集效用。硬电池约束通过状态增广处理，但为避免维数灾难，SA和CMA-ES处理硬约束，POMDP层用拉格朗日松弛转为软平均约束；并用前向分解技术将网络级POMDP分解为节点级POMDP。

Result: 相比元启发式、贪心与深度强化学习基线，FLoRa在总期望VIP上分别提升30.6%、27.8%、15.2%；节点覆盖率提升24.5%、29.2%、8.8%；成功采集率提升24.3%、25.6%、15.2%。

Conclusion: FLoRa通过多级优化与松弛分解有效解决了UAV辅助LoRa数据采集中的能量约束、占空比与决策不确定性问题，显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Data collection using Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) is challenging when LoRa IoT Devices (IoTDs) duty-cycle to conserve battery. Under energy constraints, a UAV must decide which IoTDs to visit, in what order, where to hover, and how many times to probe each node, while time-based data freshness decays. Tractably solving this problem requires a multi-level optimization architecture: discrete combinatorial optimization for routing, continuous global optimization for spatial positioning, and sequential decision-making under uncertainty. We propose FLoRa, a Flight-assisted LoRa data collection architecture using Simulated Annealing (SA) for path planning, Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy (CMA-ES) for hover positioning, and Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) for probing IoTDs. To quantify collection utility from duty-cycling nodes, we introduce the Value of Information for Pull-based systems (VIP), a metric that rewards fresh data and penalizes failed probes, imposing well-posedness and preventing indefinite probing when an IoTD is off. Tracking hard battery constraints on every POMDP sample path requires state augmentation, worsening the curse of dimensionality. For tractability, SA and CMA-ES work on the hard battery constraints, while at the POMDP layer we relax them into soft average constraints via Lagrangian relaxation. Since solving the network-wide POMDP is computationally complex, we decompose it into node-level POMDPs by approximating inter-node time dependency using a forward-decomposition technique. Evaluation shows FLoRa outperforms metaheuristic, greedy, and deep reinforcement learning baselines by 30.6%, 27.8%, and 15.2% in total expected VIP, while increasing node coverage by 24.5%, 29.2%, and 8.8%, and successful collections by 24.3%, 25.6%, and 15.2%, respectively.

</details>


### [222] [PCDT: A Predictive Cognitive Digital Twin Framework for Intelligent and Autonomous 6G Network Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09546)
*John Sengendo,Andreas Kassler,Fabrizio Granelli*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出预测性认知数字孪生(PCDT)框架，将数字孪生从被动同步副本升级为"观测—预测认知—主动控制"的闭环，用流量世界模型预测未来负载并提前分配容量，避免SLA违约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于数字孪生的网络管理多为网络的同步副本，仅在性能风险出现后才报告并触发纠偏，属于反应式控制，与认知数字孪生(CDT)及自主6G网络的愿景存在差距。

Method: 提出PCDT框架：维护一个持续更新的流量世界模型，预测近期负载，并针对预测时间窗内的峰值在SLA违约发生前分配容量，实现从观测到预测认知再到主动资源控制的闭环。

Result: 在真实流量trace上评测：PCDT分配误差最低，MAE较最佳基线(反应式控制)降低43%，RMSE降低32%；相较唯一零违约的阈值启发式，平均分配容量减少50%，重配置抖动降低56%，总运营成本降低50%。

Conclusion: PCDT推动数字孪生从被动表征转向认知、主动控制与"意图感知"的运行机制，契合自主6G网络愿景，且性能更高效。

Abstract: Future 6G networks are expected to operate as intelligent and autonomous ecosystems where monitoring, prediction, and control are integrated into continuous self-optimization loops. However, many digital-twin-based network management approaches still act mainly as synchronized replicas of the network. They observe the state, report degradation, and trigger corrective action only after performance risk has appeared. This leaves a gap between the vision of a cognitive digital twin (CDT) and the behavior of conventional reactive control loops. In this paper, we propose a Predictive Cognitive Digital Twin (PCDT) framework that closes the loop from observation to predictive cognition to proactive resource control. PCDT maintains a persistent traffic world model, forecasts near-future load, and allocates capacity for the predicted horizon peak before a Service Level Agreement (SLA) violation occurs. Evaluated on a real-world traffic trace, PCDT achieves the lowest allocation error among all benchmarked baseline frameworks, reducing MAE by 43% and RMSE by 32% relative to the best baseline (reactive control). Relative to the threshold heuristic, the only baseline with zero violations, PCDT reduces mean allocated capacity by 50%, reconfiguration churn by 56%, and total operating cost by 50%, indicating substantially more efficient performance. These results show the proposed framework advances the digital twin (DT) operation mechanism from a passive representation towards a cognitive, proactive control, and "intent-aware" mechanism aligned with autonomous 6G network vision.

</details>


### [223] [Age of Information in Queueing Systems with Merging Server Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09632)
*Lucrezia Rossi,Leonardo Badia,Andrea Munari*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分析多路径排队拓扑中AoI（信息年龄）的精确框架，重点研究split-merge（指出前人研究错误）与duplicate-merge（首次形式化分析）两种场景，并用于优化路由概率与注入速率以最小化平均AoI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多路径路由与流合并对超可靠低延迟网络和边缘计算中保持状态更新新鲜度至关重要，但已有关于split-merge的AoI分析存在错误，duplicate-merge则缺乏研究。

Method: 构建多服务器输出汇聚到单一流水线（pipeline）的多路径排队拓扑的分析框架，分别对split-merge和duplicate-merge进行建模，推导平均AoI的精确解析表达式。

Result: 证明现有split-merge研究结果不正确并给出精确解析公式；首次给出duplicate-merge场景的形式化分析；基于理论结果求解系统级优化问题。

Conclusion: 为多路径排队拓扑的AoI评估提供精确分析工具，并给出最小化平均AoI的最优随机路由概率与流量注入速率。

Abstract: We present a novel analytical framework for evaluating the age of information (AoI) in multi-path queueing topologies where output streams from multiple servers converge into a single pipeline. This theoretical scenario finds immediate application in ultra-reliable low-latency networks and edge computing architectures, where multi-path routing and stream merging can be vital strategies for maintaining fresh status updates. Specifically, we investigate two setups: a split-merge network and a duplicate-merge network. The split-merge case has been addressed in prior work, but we show that existing studies are incorrect, and our approach provides instead an exact analytical formulation. Moreover, building on similar reasoning, we provide the first formal analysis of the previously unexplored duplicate-merge scenario. Finally, we show how to leverage our theoretical results to solve key system-level optimization problems, deriving the optimal randomized routing probabilities and traffic injection rates that minimize average AoI.

</details>


### [224] [Deadline-Aware Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for TSN-Based Vehicular Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09870)
*Bernardo A. C. Pereira,Marcos Carvalho,Fatih Temiz,Shavbo Salehi,Melike Erol-Kantarci,Andreas Gavrielides,Johann M. Marquez-Barja,Daniel F. Macedo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多智能体强化学习(MARL)的队列级调度方法，用于TSN赋能的车辆边缘计算(VEC)，以应对异构动态时延需求下的网络竞争问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VEC方法常忽略共址服务间异构且动态时延需求导致的网络竞争；传统及单智能体RL调度器难以适应高度动态的车载环境和队列间依赖关系。

Method: 为每个TSN队列分配一个自主智能体，联合学习队列服务顺序与时隙时长，在速度依赖的时延约束下最小化截止期错失；采用MAPPO实现协调且自主的调度决策。

Result: 与单智能体、多智能体及非学习基线相比，MAPPO在不同流量下表现稳健；相较集中式单智能体方法，服务时延最多降低66.2%，可靠性最多提升271.8%；相比紧迫性启发式方法能实现均衡调度，且推理时间低于其他MARL方法。

Conclusion: MARL队列级调度可有效缓解TSN-VEC中的网络竞争，兼顾低时延、高可靠与均衡调度，适用于动态车载环境。

Abstract: Vehicular edge computing (VEC) enables latency-sensitive applications by bringing computing and networking resources closer to vehicles. However, existing approaches often overlook network contention among co-located services with heterogeneous and dynamic latency requirements. While time-sensitive networking (TSN) provides bounded-latency communication, conventional and reinforcement learning-based schedulers struggle to adapt to highly dynamic vehicular environments and inter-queue dependencies. To address these limitations, we propose a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) approach for queue-level scheduling in TSN-enabled VEC. Each TSN queue is assigned an autonomous agent that jointly learns the queue service order and time-slot duration to minimize deadline misses under speed-dependent latency requirements. We employ multi-agent proximal policy optimization (MAPPO) to enable coordinated yet autonomous scheduling decisions. Evaluation against single-agent, multi-agent, and non-learning-based baselines shows that MAPPO provides robust performance across different traffic profiles. Compared with centralized single-agent methods, it reduces service latency by up to 66.2% and improves reliability by up to 271.8%. Furthermore, unlike urgency-based heuristics, MAPPO ensures balanced scheduling while achieving lower inference times compared to other MARL methods.

</details>


### [225] [A Compositional Perspective on Communication-Control Co-Design for Mobile Broadband Systems Beyond 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10486)
*Angelo Vera-Rivera,Atefeh Termehchi,Ekram Hossain*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于形式化协同设计理论的组合视角方法，用于B6G网络中通信与控制子系统的协同设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B6G网络需要通信与控制子系统的协同设计，但现有以优化驱动的方法在模块化表示、需求传播追踪和设计空间可行性分析方面面临挑战。

Method: 采用形式化协同设计理论中的"组合"概念，提出组合驱动的通信-控制协同设计方法，并以无线辅助机器人控制为案例进行说明。

Result: 展示了组合视角如何应对优化驱动方法的挑战，并论证组合驱动与优化驱动方法的互补性对B6G网络的益处。

Conclusion: 识别了该方法实际应用的关键研究挑战与未来方向。

Abstract: Anticipated applications of beyond sixth-generation (B6G) mobile broadband networks will require the co-design of communication and control subsystems within the network architecture. Existing co-design approaches are predominantly optimization-driven, integrating subsystems through joint optimization problems. While this approach is effective in relatively simple systems, such formulations present challenges in (i) modular subsystem representations, (ii) tracing the propagation of requirements across subsystems, and (iii) systematically analyzing design-space feasibility, particularly as the number and complexity of interacting subsystems increase. In this article, we present a compositional perspective on co-design based on the formal theory of co-design. We examine how the notion of composition introduced by this theory can address the challenges of the conventional optimization-driven approach. Building on this perspective, we propose a composition-driven methodology for communication-control co-design in B6G networks and illustrate it through a wireless-assisted robotic control case-study. We then discuss how the composition-driven and optimization-driven co-design approaches can complement each other and why this complementarity may be beneficial for B6G networks. Finally, we identify key research challenges and future directions toward the practical adoption of the proposed methodology.

</details>


### [226] [Predictive Rerouting of Connected and Automated Vehicles Using Traffic and Charging Demand Forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10532)
*Tony Kinchen,Ting Bai,Andreas A. Malikopoulos*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出结合DCRNN预测与考虑拥堵、CO2、路程和充电需求的多目标路由策略，用于混合交通中网联自动驾驶汽车(CAV)的预测性改道，在SUMO中验证其优于多种路由方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合交通中CAV的预测性改道需兼顾交通预测与电动车充电需求，现有路由方法在拥堵缓解、排放与充电等多目标权衡上存在不足。

Method: 用DCRNN基于历史观测预测交通状况与充电需求，据此为符合条件的CAV评估可行替代路线；当替代路线保持到原目的地连通性且改善既定路线成本时接受改道。

Result: 在SUMO中设置交通中断，测试5%~45%五种CAV渗透率；相比参考情景平均行程时间指数降低约1.8%，在主动路由方法中平均行程时间指数最低、平均速度最高、总CO2排放最低；五种渗透率下共接受41次改道，K最短路径为146次、V2X主动路由为153次；参考情景总排放与行驶距离更低，体现拥堵缓解与改道额外行驶之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 所提框架能在多目标下实现更优的交通与排放表现，改道次数显著少，但拥堵缓解与额外行驶距离/排放间存在权衡。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the problem of predictive rerouting of connected and automated vehicles (CAVs) in mixed traffic with electric vehicle charging demand. We provide a framework that combines a diffusion convolutional recurrent neural network (DCRNN) with a routing policy that accounts for congestion, CO2 emissions, route length, and charging demand. The DCRNN uses historical network observations to forecast traffic conditions and charging demand. These forecasts are then used to evaluate feasible alternative routes for eligible CAVs. A route change is accepted when the alternative preserves connectivity to the original destination and improves the prescribed route cost. We evaluate the proposed framework in SUMO under controlled traffic disruptions at five CAV penetration levels, ranging from 5% to 45%. We compare its performance with K-shortest-path routing, predictive-density routing, V2X proactive routing, and a reference scenario without rerouting. In the considered scenarios, the proposed framework reduces the average travel-time index by approximately 1.8% relative to the reference scenario and achieves the lowest average travel-time index, highest average speed, and lowest aggregate CO2 emissions among the active routing methods. Across all five penetration levels, it accepts 41 route changes, compared with 146 for K-shortest-path routing and 153 for V2X proactive routing. The reference scenario retains lower aggregate emissions and distance traveled, illustrating the tradeoff between congestion reduction and the additional travel associated with rerouting.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [Mitigating Uncertainty Interactions in GenAI-based Adaptive Systems: Vision, Challenges and Preliminary Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08881)
*Karthik Vaidhyanathan,Norha Villegas,Raffaela Mirandola,Nicolás Cardozo,Ivana Dusparic,Marin Litoiu,Ralf Reussner,Gabriel Tamura*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个初步概念框架，用于刻画和缓解GenAI软件密集型系统中不确定性的交互（uncertainty interactions），强调缓解须贯穿需求、设计到运行时全生命周期，并引入“缓解函数”映射不确定性交互类别、系统设计特征、关键性、架构策略与质量属性及权衡，通过LLM服务、AI驱动运维和智能体云管理等案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI组件（LLM、智能体子系统）引入跨系统层的新型、复合不确定性来源；其概率输出、幻觉、上下文窗口限制和记忆陈旧等特性要求重新审视现有自适应系统的不确定性框架与缓解策略，尤其是不确定性发生与表现之间的交互。

Method: 提出一个初步概念框架，作为立场论文给出刻画和缓解不确定性交互的初步准则；引入“缓解函数”，将不确定性交互类别、涉及的不确定性类型、系统设计特征、关键性及缓解架构策略映射到受影响的质量属性及相应权衡；并以三个用例（LLM服务、AI驱动软件运维、智能体云管理）作为概念验证。

Result: 框架在三个 илustrative 用例上得到应用，作为概念验证展示其在LLM服务、AI驱动软件运维和智能体云管理场景中的适用性。

Conclusion: 缓解必须跨整个软件生命周期考虑（需求分析、设计决策、运行时适应），因此无法将其与执行期的监控和分析解耦；该立场论文为GenAI系统不确定性交互的刻画与缓解提供了初步概念框架和方向。

Abstract: Modern software-intensive systems increasingly incorporate GenAI components, including LLM and agentic subsystems, which introduce novel and compounding sources of uncertainty across system layers. The self-adaptive systems community has made significant strides in understanding and managing uncertainty. The intrinsic characteristics of GenAI, including probabilistic outputs, hallucinations, context window limitations, and memory staleness, demand a re-examination of existing frameworks and mitigation strategies for dealing with uncertainty, and especially, the interactions among uncertainty occurrences and manifestations. This position paper posits an initial conceptual framework that establishes preliminary guidelines for characterizing and mitigating uncertainty interactions in GenAI-based software-intensive systems. We argue that mitigation must be considered across the full software lifecycle, encompassing requirements analysis, design-time decisions, and run-time adaptations, which implies the unfeasibility of decoupling it from monitoring and analysis at execution. We introduce the notion of a mitigation function that maps uncertainty interaction categories, involved uncertainty types, system design characteristics, criticality, and mitigating architectural tactics to impacted quality attributes and corresponding tradeoffs. As a proof of concept, we apply the framework to three illustrative use cases: LLM serving, AI-driven software operations, and agentic cloud management.

</details>


### [228] [Psychological Safety in Software Engineering Teams: A Systematic Mapping Study of Team Processes and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08896)
*Adnan Manzoor,Muhammad Azeem Akbar,Jussi Kasurinen,Muhammad Hamza,Aakash Ahmad,Abdul Razzaq*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性映射研究，分析了2006年至2026年间112篇关于软件工程团队心理安全感的文献，揭示其作为多维、情境依赖的团队层面构念，与团队绩效和过程密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理安全感是软件开发团队中重要的人与行为因素，支持协作、知识共享与学习，但软件工程领域的相关证据分散在团队过程、绩效结果、情境条件与障碍等方面，缺乏系统梳理。

Method: 遵循软件工程既定指南开展系统性映射研究，对2006年至2026年6月发表的112篇原始研究，围绕六个研究问题采用描述性统计与主题分析。

Result: 心理安全感是团队层面的多维情境依赖构念，涉及人际冒险、开放沟通、协作、学习与信任；主要与生产力、软件质量、创新、团队满意度、协作、沟通和决策相关，并受领导力、组织文化、信任等因素影响。常见障碍包括沟通问题、组织约束、领导力问题、多样性与包容性挑战及工作负荷压力；尽管实践多样，但其有效性与长期可持续性的证据仍有限。

Conclusion: 心理安全感是多维、情境依赖的团队层面构念，与团队互动和绩效相关。研究强调需要在软件开发环境中开展纵向、情境敏感的心理安全感干预研究。

Abstract: Context: Psychological safety is an important human and behavioral factor in software development teams, supporting collaboration, knowledge sharing, interpersonal risk-taking, and learning. However, evidence in software engineering remains fragmented across team processes, performance outcomes, contextual conditions, barriers. Objective: This study maps literature on psychological safety in software engineering, examining its conceptualization, associations with team performance and processes, contextual influences, barriers, and strengthening strategies. Method: We conducted a systematic mapping study following established software engineering guidelines. We analyzed 112 primary studies published between 2006 and June 2026 using descriptive statistics and thematic analysis across six research questions. Results: Psychological safety emerges as a multidimensional, context-dependent, team-level construct characterized by interpersonal risk-taking, open communication, collaboration, learning, trust, and supportive interaction. It is predominantly associated with productivity, software quality, innovation, team satisfaction, collaboration, communication, and decision-making. These relationships are shaped by leadership, organizational culture, work environment, trust, and sociocultural and individual conditions. Recurring barriers include communication problems, organizational constraints, leadership issues, diversity and inclusion challenges, and workload pressures. Although diverse practices are reported, evidence of their effectiveness and long-term sustainability remains limited. Conclusion: Psychological safety is a multidimensional, context-dependent team-level construct associated with team interaction and performance. This study highlights the need for longitudinal, context-sensitive research on psychological safety interventions in software development environments.

</details>


### [229] [Evaluating Change Point Detection Methods for Software Performance Regression Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09023)
*Diego Elias Costa,Michele Tucci,Luca Traini,Daniele Di Pompeo,Thomas Bach,Francois Farquet,David Daly,Simon Eismann,Petr Tůma,Vittorio Cortellessa,André van Hoorn*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文全面评估了多种变点检测（CPD）方法在真实软件性能测量数据上的有效性，通过采集三大软件系统的数据、人工标注性能变化并进行一致性分析，对比了12种CPD方法的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性能问题会侵蚀用户信任、违反服务级别协议并影响业务效率，因此需要在开发周期尽早检测性能回归。CPD方法虽已在金融、医疗等领域广泛用于自动识别变化，但其在软件性能测量数据上的有效性尚未得到充分评估。

Method: 首先从三个大型软件系统采集性能测量数据并刻画性能时间序列的独有特性；随后进行大规模人工标注工作，评估标注者识别潜在性能变化的一致性；最后评估12种不同的CPD方法在检测潜在性能变化方面的准确性。

Result: 通过对比12种CPD方法，获得了它们在软件性能回归分析中检测潜在性能变化的准确性表现，并揭示了其适用性与有效性方面的洞见。

Conclusion: 该研究为CPD方法在软件性能回归分析中的适用性和有效性提供了系统性洞见，有助于指导实践中选择合适的方法进行早期性能回归检测。

Abstract: Performance issues in software systems are a critical quality issue that can erode user trust, violate service-level agreements, and ultimately affect business efficiency. Consequently, software performance engineering has shifted its focus to developing robust techniques to detect performance regressions as early as possible in the development cycle. Performance regression analysis often relies on time series of performance measurements to detect significant changes in performance behavior. Change Point Detection (CPD) methods have been widely used to automate the identification of such changes in various domains, including finance, healthcare, and performance monitoring. However, the effectiveness of these methods for software performance measurements has not been thoroughly evaluated. In this paper, we present a comprehensive study to evaluate the effectiveness of various CPD methods on real-world software performance measurement datasets. We start by collecting performance measurement data from three large software systems and characterizing the unique properties of performance time series data. Then, we undertake a large-scale effort to annotate and evaluate the consistency of human annotators' identification of potential performance changes. Thereafter, we evaluate the accuracy of twelve distinct CPD methods in detecting potential performance changes, providing insights into their applicability and effectiveness in software performance regression analysis.

</details>


### [230] [Large-scale Repository Engineering via Agent-Native Reusable Code Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09079)
*Haibo Jin,Peng Kuang,Xucheng Yu,Jerry Wang,Dehao Wu,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出"Code Primitives"（带接口契约、依赖闭包、验证测试与溯源的可复用可执行组件）及LEGO框架，构建1424个组件的CodeFace库与522任务的LEGO-REPO基准，平均提升13个模型0.1474分，GPT-5.6-terra提升61.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽已从函数级代码生成走向仓库级构建，但构建完整仓库仍困难：模块、接口、配置、测试与依赖需协同工作，现有方法缺乏可复用、可验证的构建单元。

Method: 提出Code Primitives（agent原生的可复用可执行组件，含接口契约、依赖闭包、验证测试与溯源），每个原语用常驻LLM评估相关性并适配实现、接口和依赖；构建CodeFace库（1,424个验证原语）。提出LEGO框架：激活任务相关原语，将适配实现与任务代码集成并解决跨组件约束，再依据执行测试迭代修订。

Result: 构建LEGO-REPO基准：522个可执行重建任务、7个领域、22条能力轨道、5个难度级别，以原生测试套件评分。13个骨干模型中最高交付分仅0.318，41.0%任务得零分；LEGO平均提升全部13个模型0.1474，GPT-5.6-terra从0.3180升至0.5134（+61.4%）。适配原语优于检索代码作上下文或原样vendor；效果在独立仓库agent、三个外部基准及重新挖掘的CodeFace上均保持；GPT-OSS-20B适配与诊断以低24.0%成本保留95.1%同质分数。

Conclusion: agent原生可复用代码原语能显著提升仓库级代码构建能力，效果具泛化性与成本效益，为仓库尺度自动化构建提供可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models equipped with development environments have moved code generation toward repository-scale construction, yet building complete repositories remains difficult because interacting modules, interfaces, configurations, tests, and dependencies must work together. We introduce Code Primitives, agent-native reusable executable components with interface contracts, dependency closures, validation tests, and provenance. Each primitive uses a resident LLM to assess relevance and adapt its implementation, interfaces, and dependencies to the target repository, and we organize 1,424 validated primitives in CodeFace, a searchable library for repository construction. We introduce LEGO (Large-scale repository Engineering via aGent-native reusable cOde primitives), which activates task-relevant primitives, integrates their adapted implementations with task-specific code while resolving cross-component constraints, and revises the result against executed tests. To measure construction end to end, we build LEGO-REPO, a benchmark of 522 executable reconstruction tasks spanning seven software domains, 22 capability tracks, and five difficulty levels, scored against native test suites between an empty-package floor and original-source ceiling. The strongest of 13 evaluated backbones reaches a delivery score of 0.318 and scores zero on 41.0% of tasks; LEGO improves all 13 by 0.1474 on average and raises GPT-5.6-terra from 0.3180 to 0.5134 (+61.4%). In controlled comparisons, adapted primitives outperform retrieved code supplied as context or vendored unchanged. The effect persists against independent repository agents, across three external benchmarks, and with a disjointly re-mined CodeFace; GPT-OSS-20B for adaptation and diagnosis retains 95.1% of the homogeneous score at 24.0% lower cost.

</details>


### [231] [Who Broke Me? Execution-Guided Repair of Behavioral Dependency Breaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09267)
*Juntao Chen,Tingting Bi,Yanlin Wang,Patanamon Thongtanunam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出BBCFixer方法，通过在新旧库版本下运行测试、对比返回值差异定位根API，并用其过滤库diff，帮助LLM修复依赖升级导致的行为破坏性变更；同时构建BBCBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖升级可能在不改变库接口的情况下破坏下游项目（行为破坏性变更），失败测试不一定指向根API，且此类破坏无编译器反馈、常缺乏文档，现有LLM修复方法难以获取有效升级证据，也无法自动检索上游证据。

Method: BBCFixer：在新旧库版本下运行失败测试，对返回值不同的调用进行排序以识别候选根API，再用该候选过滤库diff以获取相关上游证据，供LLM代理修复。并构建BBCBench基准，包含Python和JavaScript中100个行为依赖破坏案例。

Result: 在BBCBench上与两个基线（Pure Agent、BDUpdater）比较：BBCFixer平均通过率最高提升17%，且每次成功修复所需代理步数减少最多27%。

Conclusion: BBCFixer能有效选择行为破坏背后的上游证据，使LLM代理更高效、更有效地修复行为破坏性变更。

Abstract: Dependency upgrades can break downstream projects without changing the library interface. Such behavioral breaking changes are difficult for developers to fix, because the failing test does not always point to the root API, the upstream API that causes the break. Existing LLM-based repair methods obtain evidence for the repair from compiler feedback or library documentation. However, a behavioral break produces no compiler feedback and is often undocumented. Agents that receive no upgrade evidence also usually do not retrieve upstream evidence themselves, and most of their failed repairs do not identify the root API.
  The library diff provides useful upstream evidence, but the root API must first be identified to select the relevant part of the diff. We present BBCFixer, a repair method that runs the failing test under the old and new library versions, ranks the calls whose return value differs to identify a candidate root API, and filters the library diff with that candidate. To evaluate repairs of behavioral breaks, we further introduce BBCBench, a benchmark of 100 behavioral dependency breaks in Python and JavaScript.
  We compare BBCFixer on BBCBench with two baselines: (1) Pure Agent, an agent that receives no upgrade evidence, and (2) BDUpdater, a method that mines library documentation. BBCFixer raises the average pass rate by up to 17% relative to Pure Agent and needs up to 27% fewer agent steps per successful repair. BBCFixer thus provides a way to select the upstream evidence behind a behavioral break, so that an LLM agent can repair behavioral breaking changes more effectively and efficiently.

</details>


### [232] [Beyond FAIR: A Fitness Function Framework for Sustainable Research Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09580)
*Irdin Pekaric,Philipp Zech*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文将研究软件可持续性的评估从FAIR原则扩展到环境责任与安全两个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FAIR原则虽重要，但未覆盖所有可持续性关切。研究软件还应具备环境责任性（过度资源消耗增加环境成本）和长期安全性（不安全软件带来维护开销、技术债务和复用障碍）。

Method: 扩展先前提出的可持续研究软件适应度函数框架，引入两组新的适应度函数：面向资源效率与执行足迹的环境函数，以及面向依赖健康、漏洞暴露与安全配置的安全函数。

Result: 这些函数将持续软件评估从FAIR合规性拓宽为更完整的可持续性视图，使研究软件可持续性被视为包含FAIR、环境责任和长期安全的多维属性。

Conclusion: 研究软件可持续性是涵盖FAIR、环境责任与长期安全的多维特性，扩展后的适应度函数框架支持更全面的持续评估。

Abstract: Research software sustainability is often assessed through the FAIR principles: findability, accessibility, interoperability and reusability. While important, FAIR does not cover all relevant sustainability concerns. Research software should also be environmentally responsible and secure over time. Excessive resource consumption increases environmental cost, while insecure software creates maintenance overhead, technical debt and barriers to reuse. To this end, in this paper, we extend a previously proposed fitness function framework for sustainable research software beyond FAIR. We introduce two additional sets of fitness functions: environmental functions targeting resource efficiency and execution footprint, and security functions targeting dependency health, vulnerability exposure and secure configuration. Together, these functions broaden continuous software assessment from FAIR compliance to a more complete view of sustainability. The resulting framework treats research software sustainability as a multidimensional property that includes FAIR, environmental responsibility and long-term security.

</details>


### [233] [GRAML: Graph-Grounded Reasoning and Multi-Task Learning for LLM-Based Software Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09605)
*Ruohan Li,Miaoqing Tian,Haipeng Qu,Ruobing Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GRAML框架结合图证据、漏洞描述生成与多任务训练，提升LLM漏洞检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在软件漏洞检测中对控制流和数据流信息利用不足，限制其性能。

Method: 对C/C++程序做静态分析，提取关键源码行及带类型的行关系作为结构证据；用该证据引导GPT-5执行ToT-VR过程生成漏洞描述；将描述与检测、定位、评估样本结合，构建统一四任务训练数据集。

Result: 在ID测试集和六个OOD数据集上，GRAML平均F1为66.67%–68.70%，优于SOTA基线最高30.92%。消融实验表明ToT-VR和图引导漏洞描述优于标准CoT推理和原始CPG序列化。

Conclusion: GRAML为构建更可靠、安全的LLM软件工程系统提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been widely applied to software vulnerability detection. However, their performance is often limited by insufficient use of control-flow and data-flow information. In this paper, we propose GRAML, a framework that combines graph evidence, vulnerability description generation, and multi-task training. GRAML first performs static analysis on C/C++ programs to extract critical source lines and typed line relations as structural evidence. It then uses this evidence to guide GPT-5 through the Tree-of-Thought-guided Vulnerability Reasoning (ToT-VR) process and generate vulnerability descriptions. These descriptions are further combined with Detection, Localization, and Assessment samples to build a unified four-task training dataset. We evaluate GRAML on an in-distribution (ID) test set and six out-of-distribution (OOD) datasets. The results show that GRAML achieves average F1 scores ranging from 66.67% to 68.70%, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines by up to 30.92%. Ablation experiments further show that ToT-VR and graph-guided vulnerability descriptions improve detection performance compared with standard Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning and raw Code Property Graph (CPG) serializations. These findings provide practical guidance for building more reliable and secure software engineering systems with large language models.

</details>


### [234] [A Chat Assistant for Software Exploration in a 3D Software Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09901)
*Malte Hansen,Karim Issa,Wilhelm Hasselbring*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个嵌入ExplorViz 3D软件可视化工具中的聊天助手，基于LLM与CopilotKit实现，支持自然语言问答与可视化操作，经11人实验评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低探索性程序理解任务中的交互开销，让用户通过自然语言与软件可视化系统交互。

Method: 基于LLM和CopilotKit库构建聊天助手，结合概率性LLM与确定性、基于工具的操作（类似MCP），支持问答、触发可视化动作及重构软件系统结构（增删改）。

Result: 11人实验中，生成的摘要与解释被评为大体正确；高亮实体、创建配色主题等操作可用性高；但开放式聊天辅助的软件重构结果不一，需更强LLM护栏与用户反馈整合。

Conclusion: 该助手整体被认为可用且有前景，可减少程序理解中的交互开销，并提供视频与复现包。

Abstract: We present a chat assistant for interactive software exploration, embedded in the 3D software visualization tool ExplorViz. The assistant builds upon current Large Language Models (LLMs) and enables users to ask questions about the currently visualized software system and trigger actions that change the visualization through natural language. We integrate the chat assistant in our ExplorViz frontend using the CopilotKit libraries such that probabilistic LLMs are combined with deterministic and tool-based actions similar to implementations using the Model Context Protocol (MCP). The chat assistant is also enabled to restructure the software system by adding, removing, or modifying parts of the software system in the visualization. An empirical experiment with eleven participants evaluated both perceived comprehension support and the tool calls that were triggered by the chat assistant. Participants rated the assistant's generated summaries and explanations as largely correct. They also reported high usability for actions like highlighting entities in the visualization and the creation of new color themes. In contrast, open-ended chat-assisted software restructuring in the visualization showed mixed results. This suggests a need for stronger guardrails for the employed LLM and better incorporation of user feedback. Overall, the assistant was perceived as usable and promising for reducing interaction overhead during exploratory program comprehension tasks. We provide a video presenting the chat assistant's use and a reproduction package of the software system that was used in our evaluation.

</details>


### [235] [Analysis and Visualization of the Linux Kernel's Software Evolution Using the City Metaphor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09910)
*Malte Hansen,Lukas Damerau,Moritz Bugla,David Moreno-Lumbreras,Wilhelm Hasselbring*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出用Exploviz工具基于3D城市隐喻可视化Linux内核结构及代码演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux内核代码量超4000万行，理解其内部结构与软件演化是巨大挑战，需要有效的可视化手段。

Method: 用自定义分析服务分析Linux Git仓库提交，Web前端采用3D城市隐喻展示软件结构；收集每文件代码行数等指标并累积到目录和提交级别，将指标映射到建筑尺寸或热图；提供可视化、搜索、过滤等交互功能。

Result: 展示了两类可视化：内核仓库全部文件的可视化，以及Linux内核中Rust文件代码演化的可视分析。

Conclusion: 该方案可支持对大规模Linux内核结构和演化进行交互式探索，并附带演示视频。

Abstract: The Linux kernel is one of the largest and longest-maintained open source projects in existence. With more than 40 million lines of code, understanding the kernel's internal structure and assessing its software evolution is a great challenge. In this paper, we present an approach to visualize the Linux kernel using our software visualization tool ExplorViz. We analyze commits from the Linux Git repository using a custom analysis service. The web-based frontend utilizes the 3D city metaphor for visualization of the software structure. Metrics such as the number of lines are collected for each file and are accumulated for directories and commits. Calculated metrics can be mapped to the building's dimensions or be displayed via a heat map. A wide range of visualization, search, and filter options enable the interactive exploration of the visualized data. We present both a visualization of all files in the kernel repository and a visual analysis of the code evolution of Rust files in the Linux kernel. Demo video: https://youtu.be/3ODnERqD8g0

</details>


### [236] [AgentTracer: Tracing Indirect Prompt Injection Attack through Fine-Grained Intention-Execution Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09935)
*Zitong Yao,Jiangrong Wu,Yixi Lin,Anrui Huang,Luoyun Zhang,Yuhong Nan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AgentTracer是一个意图感知的追踪框架，将间接提示注入(IPI)视为任务意图漂移，通过构建意图驱动执行图重建攻击链并定位注入源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent与外部资源交互完成复杂任务时面临间接提示注入(IPI)威胁，恶意指令会重定向agent至攻击者意图任务。由于IPI在真实环境中难以防御，事后追踪至关重要，但现有方法仅捕获显式控制流和数据流依赖，忽视了恶意指令驱动的工具调用间隐式关系，导致攻击链难以完整重建。

Method: AgentTracer恢复工具调用间的隐式决策依赖，构建意图驱动执行图，将分散的工具调用按任务意图连接。它结合用户请求与操作知识库构建用户意图授权空间，识别意图漂移的工具调用。从待审计的异常工具调用出发，执行基于目标的剪枝和反向追踪，重建攻击链并定位注入源和注入点。

Result: 在包含AgentDyn和InjecAgent成功IPI攻击日志及1800个用户请求、9000条无IPI背景工具调用的正常日志的端到端实验中，AgentTracer达到94.17%注入点准确率和93.56%路径精度。对比实验显示，注入点准确率较现有方法提升18%至54%。

Conclusion: AgentTracer通过意图感知的追踪方法有效解决了现有依赖关系追踪的局限，能准确重建IPI攻击链并定位注入点，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents interact with external resources to complete complex user tasks, exposing them to indirect prompt injection (IPI), where malicious instructions redirect agents toward attacker-intended tasks. Since IPI is difficult to defend against in real-world environments, post-incident tracing is essential for locating the injection source and reconstructing the attack chain. However, existing tracing methods primarily capture explicit control-flow and data-flow dependencies, overlooking the implicit relationships among tool calls driven by the malicious instruction. These tool calls may lack explicit dependencies and be interleaved with legitimate operations, making complete attack-chain reconstruction difficult. In this paper, we present AgentTracer, an intent-aware tracing framework that treats IPI as task intent drift. AgentTracer recovers implicit decision dependencies among tool calls to construct an Intent-Driven Execution Graph that connects dispersed tool calls by task intent. It combines the user request with an operation knowledge base to construct a user intent authorization space and identify intent-drift tool calls. Starting from an anomalous tool call under audit, AgentTracer performs target-based pruning and backward tracing to reconstruct the attack chain and locate the injection source and injection point. To evaluate AgentTracer in the presence of noise from normal tasks, we combine execution logs constructed from successful IPI attacks in AgentDyn and InjecAgent with normal execution logs containing 1,800 user requests and 9,000 background tool calls without IPI. In end-to-end experiments, AgentTracer achieves 94.17 percent injection-point accuracy and 93.56 percent path precision. Comparative experiments show that AgentTracer improves injection-point accuracy over existing methods by 18 to 54 percent.

</details>


### [237] [Designing Collaborative AI-Driven Workflows for Scientific Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09995)
*Akash Dhruv,Max Knobbe,Pedro Machado,Anshu Dubey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出将智能体AI嵌入协作团队而非全自动化的科学计算工作流：专家负责规范与规划，智能体在确定性编排下写代码，每阶段数值比对加人工审核。以将大型高能物理Fortran程序翻译为C++为案例，跨14组实验发现简单的"作者-审阅"循环在约三分之一成本下完成相当数量的文件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI已能执行软件工程与科研中的广泛任务，但在科学计算中，难点在于验证智能体生成的代码是否既正确又能被跨领域团队理解。因此作者认为不应把系统当作完全自动化，而应嵌入协作团队结构。

Method: 领域专家编写规范和计划，智能体在确定性编排模式下生成目标代码；每个阶段以数值比对参照代码收尾，并需人工审核批准才进入下一阶段。以大型高能物理应用从Fortran到C++的翻译为任务，在不同编排器、设计模式和模型下运行14组实验。

Result: 在14组实验中，一个带强制限制的简单"作者-审阅"循环所完成的文件数量与多智能体工作流相当，但每文件成本约为后者的三分之一。

Conclusion: 在科学计算代码生成中，协作式、人工把关的简单工作流在成本效率上优于更复杂的多智能体编排，应作为智能体系统的主要使用范式。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence systems can carry out a broad range of tasks in software engineering and scientific research, from writing and translating code to running workflows for data analysis and visualization. In scientific computing, the difficulty is verifying that agent-generated code is both correct and understandable to teams whose members bring different areas of expertise. We therefore argue that these systems are best used within collaborative team structures rather than as full automation. In the workflows we propose, domain experts write the specification and plan, and agents operate under a deterministic orchestration pattern to write the target code. Each stage ends with a numerical comparison against the reference code and requires human review and approval before the next begins. We evaluate these workflows on the translation of a large high-energy physics application from Fortran to C++, running the same task under different orchestrators, design patterns, and models. Across fourteen experiments, a simple author--reviewer loop with enforced limits completed a comparable number of files to a multi-agent workflow at about one-third of the cost per file.

</details>


### [238] [AdaT$^2$: Adaptive Test Transformations for Black-Box Boundary Testing of Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10141)
*Liting Lin,Boxi Yu,Qinghua Xu,Yuzhong Zhang,Lionel Briand,Emir Muñoz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AdaT²：通过探索对话提取策略条件语句，用语句引导边界测试生成，结合自适应对选择（Bayes-UCB）与变换指令，在τ³-bench上生成更多有效边界测试并检测更多策略故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM对话代理必须遵守策略，而策略中每个条件都在用户请求空间上划定边界，代理需在两侧表现出不同行为，因此需要系统地测试这些边界。现有方法（如AgentEval）在生成有效边界测试方面覆盖不足。

Method: 从代理在探索性对话（由LLM扮演用户）中的回复里提取语句，每条语句描述一个条件及该条件成立时的期望行为。用单语句生成普通测试，用"语句+测试变换指令"（如"省略一个必需输入"）生成变换测试，指令可将测试移到边界的另一侧或移到其他边界。面对指数级多的语句-指令对，采用自适应选择：语句按新颖性选择，指令用Bayes-UCB bandit算法选择，并根据测试是否生成、是否被LLM裁判判定通过进行学习。

Result: 在τ³-bench四个领域上，AdaT²有62.7%–83.3%的测试为有效边界测试（期望行为由显式定义的），每个领域都高于AgentEval（47.7%–68.1%）。变换测试额外发现了13–46个普通测试遗漏的显式定义边界。作为回归测试，AdaT²在airline领域检测出全部8个植入策略故障，在retail领域检测出8个中的4个；而AgentEval测试数不到三分之一，仅分别检测出5个和2个。

Conclusion: AdaT²通过语句提取、变换引导和自适应对选择，能更全面地生成有效边界测试，并作为回归测试更有效地检测策略故障，优于AgentEval。

Abstract: Conversational agents based on large language models (LLMs) must comply with policies. Each condition in a policy draws a boundary between user requests, and the agent must behave differently on its two sides. We present AdaT$^2$, which extracts statements from the agent's replies in exploratory conversations with an LLM acting as the user, and uses the statements to guide boundary test generation. Each statement describes one condition and the behavior expected when the condition holds. Besides plain tests guided by single statements, AdaT$^2$ writes transformed tests guided by pairs of a statement and a test transformation instruction, such as "omit one required input". The instruction of a pair can move a test to the other side of the statement's boundary or to another boundary. The statements and instructions form far more pairs than a run can try, and many pairs are not applicable. Adaptive pair selection therefore chooses the statement of each pair by novelty and the instruction with the bandit algorithm Bayes-UCB, which learns from whether earlier pairs yielded a test and whether the agent passed it according to an LLM judge. Our benchmark counts the two sides of each boundary separately and distinguishes boundaries explicitly defined by the agent's prompt, tool code, or knowledge base from boundaries that the agent's LLM infers from domain knowledge. On four domains of $τ^3$-bench, 62.7% to 83.3% of AdaT$^2$'s tests are valid boundary tests whose expected behavior is explicitly defined, higher in every domain than AgentEval's (47.7% to 68.1%). Transformed tests add 13 to 46 explicitly defined boundaries that plain tests miss. As regression tests, AdaT$^2$'s test suites detect all eight seeded policy faults in the airline domain and four of eight in the retail domain, and AgentEval's test suites, with fewer than a third as many tests, detect five and two, respectively.

</details>


### [239] [Why Software Engineering Is Indispensable in the Age of Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10226)
*Alfonso Fuggetta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文论证AI编码代理的兴起使软件工程和软件工程师变得不可或缺而非多余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨AI是否能取代软件工程学科及软件工程师这一核心问题。

Method: 通过分析大语言模型的三个结构性特性（概率生成、不可知论、语义无状态）构建论证框架。

Result: 发现LLM的结构特性造成无法通过训练消除的"结构性真空"，需四种知识杠杆填补。

Conclusion: 方法论、领域、设计、流程四类知识须被固化为持久化制品，软件工程师作为方法论者、协调者和守护者不可或缺。

Abstract: Can AI make Software Engineering (SE) -- the discipline -- obsolete? And can it make software engineers -- the professionals -- redundant? This paper argues that the rise of capable AI coding agents makes SE and software engineers essential, not obsolete: the missing foundation without which AI-assisted development produces misleadingly plausible, unverifiable, and ultimately untrustworthy software. Three structural properties of large language models (probabilistic generation, agnosticism, and semantic statelessness) create a structural vacuum that no amount of training can eliminate. Filling it requires four knowledge levers: methodological knowledge, domain knowledge, design choices, and process choices. All four must be reified as persistent artifacts, and each requires the software engineer as methodologist, mediator, and custodian.

</details>


### [240] [TestGRAD: Evolving Test Suites via Failure Pattern Momentum for SWE-Agent Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10242)
*Pengfei He,Jiayuan Zhou,Shaowei Wang,Ruiqi Pan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出TestGRAD框架，将测试生成形式化为测试空间优化，通过差分损失、全CRUD梯度与失败模式动量提升集成补丁选择能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWE-agent集成依赖基于测试的补丁选择，但现有测试生成方法缺乏显式选择损失、优化方向不完整、且缺少失败反馈。

Method: 借鉴带动量的梯度下降，设计三个概念：差分损失（按行为区分候选补丁）、全CRUD梯度（读/增/改/删测试）、失败模式动量（挖掘失败序列避免重复方向并压缩上下文）。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，4-agent集成达84.2% Pass@1，超过最强基线80.6%达3.6个百分点，失败历史上下文压缩超100倍。

Conclusion: TestGRAD作为自动测试优化框架，有效提升补丁选择与问题解决率，并大幅降低上下文开销。

Abstract: SWE-agent ensembles improve issue resolution by combining candidate patches from different agents with complementary strengths. The central problem is therefore test-based selection: generate tests, execute candidate patches, and identify the best patch. We formulate this process as test-space optimization: evolving an executable repository test suite until it distinguishes competing patches. Existing test-generation methods are limited optimizers. They usually lack an explicit loss for ensemble selection, optimize through incomplete directions that mostly create new tests or delete old ones, and perform one-off generation without feedback from repeated failures. Inspired by gradient descent with momentum, we introduce TestGRAD, a framework for automatic test optimization. TestGRAD centers on three concepts. Differential loss gives the optimizer an explicit execution-defined target: useful tests should separate candidate patches by behavior. Full CRUD gradients expand the update direction from merely creating or deleting tests to reading existing test infrastructure, creating new tests, updating stale assertions, and deleting only obsolete tests. Failure Pattern Momentum mines frequent failure sequences from memory, allowing the optimizer to avoid repeated non-discriminative directions while compressing the failure-history context. On SWE-bench Verified, TestGRAD achieves 84.2% Pass@1 with a 4-agent ensemble, outperforming the strongest baseline (80.6%) by an absolute improvement of 3.6 percentage points, while compressing failure-history context by over $100\times$.

</details>


### [241] [QuSema: Detecting Silent Bugs in Quantum Libraries via Quantum-knowledge-enhanced Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10258)
*Yujin Song,Kaining Zhang,Qixin Zhang,Shuai Wang,Pingchuan Ma,Yuxuan Du*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 QuSema，一种基于量子语义与文档约束的自主测试智能体，用于发现量子库中的静默 bug，在 Qiskit 和 PennyLane 上验证并发现 40 个新 bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子库已成为量子算法开发的关键基础设施，但现有测试方法主要依赖失败型或对比型预言机，只有在执行失败、违反运行时检查或与其他实现不一致时才暴露 bug。当缺少合适的执行型预言机时，静默 bug 难以被检测，可能导致错误结果传播到实验结论、仿真研究和算法设计中。

Method: QuSema 将量子语义与文档约束作为源码级语义预言机，判断实现逻辑是否会从合法输入产生非法输出。它通过智能体循环反复检查库 API 文档与源码，推理量子操作的预期行为，识别潜在语义偏差，并通过库 API 生成可执行测试进行验证，将高层行为不匹配转化为具体的用户可触发 bug 报告。

Result: 作者为 Qiskit 和 PennyLane 实现了 QuSema。在 20 个历史静默 bug 基准上，QuSema 的平均 bug 重定位数量高于 Claude Code 和 Codex，且 DeepSeek 配置成本低于 Claude Code。QuSema 还发现 40 个此前未知并被开发者确认的 bug，其中 30 个为静默 bug。

Conclusion: QuSema 通过量子语义驱动的源码级语义预言机有效发现量子库中的静默 bug，突破了传统执行型预言机的局限，并展现出优于通用代码智能体的 bug 定位能力和更高的成本效益。

Abstract: Quantum libraries are now critical infrastructure for quantum algorithm development, yet their correctness remains difficult to test. Existing testing techniques mainly rely on failure-based or comparison-based oracles, exposing bugs only when executions fail, violate runtime checks, or disagree with another implementation. Their applicability is limited when suitable execution-based oracles are unavailable, leaving some silent bugs undetected. Such missed bugs can produce incorrect results that propagate into experimental conclusions, simulation studies, and algorithmic designs. Here we present QuSema, an autonomous testing agent for finding silent bugs in quantum libraries. QuSema uses constraints from quantum semantics and documentation as a source-level semantic oracle to assess whether implementation logic can produce invalid outputs from valid inputs. It operates through an agentic loop that repeatedly inspects library API documentation and source code, reasons about the intended behavior of quantum operations, identifies potential semantic deviations, and validates them by generating executable tests through library APIs. Guided by quantum-domain reasoning, QuSema turns high-level behavioral mismatches into concrete, user-triggerable bug reports, enabling it to uncover non-crash defects. We implement QuSema for Qiskit and PennyLane. On a benchmark of 20 historical silent bugs, QuSema achieves higher mean bug relocation counts than Claude Code and Codex, with the DeepSeek configuration costing less than Claude Code. QuSema also discovers 40 previously unknown bugs confirmed by the developers, including 30 silent bugs.

</details>


### [242] [Using Small Language Models to Reverse-Engineer Machine Learning Pipelines Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10261)
*Nicolas Lacroix,Frederic Precioso,Mireille Blay-Fornarino,Sebastien Mosser*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究评估小型语言模型（SLMs）能否利用代码理解与分类能力，从源码中提取ML流水线阶段，以克服现有方法在可扩展性和领域多样性上的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从源码中提取ML流水线阶段有助于理解ML实践，但ML领域（算法、数据集）不断演进带来多样性，现有方法要么依赖不可扩展的人工标注，要么使用无法良好支持领域多样性的分类器，因此需要更可靠的方案。

Method: 基于两项代表性前人工作开展验证性研究：先用Cochran's Q检验比较多个SLM，再用两项McNemar检验将最佳模型与参考研究对比；另用Cochran's Q检验考察分类体系措辞变化对SLM表现的影响；最后用拟合优度检验比较SLM分类与前人研究的ML实践洞察。

Result: 分类体系措辞显著影响分类性能；最佳SLM结果良好但未超越其他分类器；三种分类方法得出的数据科学家实践洞察存在显著差异且效应量不同。

Conclusion: 现有分类方法的局限会偏倚我们对ML实践的理解；当前SLM在无需微调下表现可期，但仍存在常见局限，且推理成本高，影响其在大规模研究中的适用性。

Abstract: Context: Once defined a taxonomy of stages structuring Machine Learning (ML) pipelines (e.g. Data Preprocessing, Modeling...), extracting these stages from source code is key for better understanding ML practices. However, the diversity caused by the constant evolution of ML (e.g., algorithms, datasets) makes this task challenging. Existing approaches either rely on non-scalable manual labeling or on classifiers that do not properly support domain's diversity. These limitations call for more reliable solutions.
  Objective: We evaluate whether Small Language Models (SLMs) can leverage their code understanding and classification abilities to address these limitations, and enhance our understanding of practices in ML.
  Method: We conduct a confirmatory study based on two relevant reference works representing current limitations in the state-of-the-art. We first compare several SLMs using Cochran's Q test, then evaluate the best-performing model against reference studies via two McNemar's tests. An additional Cochran's Q test examines how taxonomy definition variations affect the SLM performance. Finally, goodness-of-fit tests compare ML practice insights from SLM classification with those from prior studies.
  Results: First, we found that the taxonomy wording significantly impacts classification performance. Second, the best performing SLM yielded good results, yet, without outperforming other classifiers. Third, the three classification methods led to significantly different insights, with varying effect sizes, when exploring practices of data scientists.
  Conclusions: Limitations of existing classification methods bias our understanding of ML practices. While current SLMs show promising results without prior fine-tuning, they still exhibit common limitations, in addition to inference high costs challenging their applicability in large-scale studies.

</details>


### [243] [When Sub-Agents Work in Parallel: The Promises and Pitfalls of Dynamic Concurrency in Long-Horizon Coding Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10263)
*Han Li,HanHaoNing Li,Ziqian Jiang,Yiling Lou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究动态并发（dynamic concurrency）作为编码智能体执行策略，通过对比Codex、Claude Code、Kimi Code启用/禁用该策略的匹配执行，覆盖354个任务、2124次执行，评估其端到端效果、调度行为及13种并发特有失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体从有限软件工程任务迈向长时程开发，动态并发有望扩展复杂开发任务。然而现有研究多聚焦短任务的智能体失败或预定义多智能体工作流协作，对前沿智能体跨任务复杂度下的动态并发缺乏洞察。

Method: 将动态并发作为执行策略，对Codex、Claude Code、Kimi Code进行启用/禁用的匹配对照实验；智能体在执行中决定是否及如何生成并发子智能体。覆盖354个任务、2124次执行，涵盖不同任务复杂度与执行时程。

Result: 评估了动态并发的端到端影响与调度行为，通过匹配轨迹分析归纳出13种并发特有失败模式、28种可观察模式，并识别出并发带来优势的条件。

Conclusion: 动态并发在长时程开发中使编排成为任务完成的核心；模型能力主导短任务结果，而动态并发的价值取决于具体条件，需通过实证理解其失败模式与适用场景。

Abstract: As coding agents advance from bounded software engineering tasks toward long horizon development, dynamic concurrency offers a promising way to scale complex development tasks. Under this policy, agents decide during execution whether and how to spawn concurrent sub-agents. Model capability largely determines outcomes on shorter tasks, whereas long horizon development makes orchestration central to task completion. Existing work, focused on coding agent failures on shorter tasks or collaboration in predefined multiagent workflows, offers little insight into dynamic concurrency in frontier agents across task complexity. We study dynamic concurrency as an execution policy through controlled comparisons of matched Codex, Claude Code, and Kimi Code executions with the policy enabled or disabled. Across 354 tasks and 2,124 executions spanning a range of task complexities and execution horizons, we evaluate its end to end effects and scheduling behavior, and analyze matched trajectories to characterize 13 concurrency specific failure modes, 28 observable patterns, and the conditions under which it provides an advantage.

</details>


### [244] [TaoD2C-Bench: Benchmarking MLLMs for Industrial UI Code Generation Beyond Visual Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10374)
*Chengwei Shi,Yunnong Chen,Tingting Zhou,Qiang Lu,Shiyu Yue,Xinyuan Hu,Jianfang Ru,Liuqing Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出工业级设计到代码（D2C）基准TaoD2C-Bench，包含2861个真实设计及97652条专家标注，评估MLLM在约束感知跨模态推理与UI代码生成上的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型需从视觉识别进阶到约束感知的跨模态推理，而工业UI设计到代码场景要求结合设计图与杂乱图层元数据、按目标库约束实现代码，但该能力缺乏现实工业场景的评估。

Method: 构建TaoD2C数据集（17个商业平台的2861个生产设计，四类标注：组件、分组、对齐、位置，区分必需约束与允许的实现选择），并定义三个评测任务：端到端UI代码生成、需求推断、需求实现，对八个MLLM进行评估。

Result: 八个MLLM在满足实现需求的UI代码生成上存在显著差距，在推断与实现上呈现不同性能特征；视觉重建能力并不必然意味着能生成满足需求的代码。

Conclusion: TaoD2C-Bench揭示了MLLM在工业UI代码生成中的不足，并开源数据集以支持相关研究。

Abstract: A key challenge for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is moving beyond visual recognition to constraint-aware cross-modal reasoning. This involves combining visual cues with information from other modalities to understand elements' relationships under domain-specific rules. This challenge is acutely evident in industrial design-to-code (D2C), which converts user interface (UI) designs into code and requires MLLMs to connect design images with disorganized layer metadata, infer component and layout implementation requirements, and realize them in code under target-library constraints. However, these capabilities remain insufficiently evaluated in realistic industrial settings. To fill this gap, we present TaoD2C-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating MLLMs' ability to generate UI code that satisfies implementation requirements in industrial applications. The TaoD2C dataset consists of 2,861 production designs from 17 commercial platforms with 97,652 expert annotations across four categories: Component, Group, Alignment, and Position. These annotations distinguish required constraints from permitted implementation choices. TaoD2C-Bench defines three tasks: end-to-end UI code generation, requirement inference, and requirement realization. Evaluating eight MLLMs reveals substantial gaps in generating UI code that satisfies implementation requirements, alongside distinct performance profiles in inference and realization. We further show that MLLMs' visual reconstruction ability does not necessarily imply an ability to generate code that meets these requirements. We release TaoD2C to support research on industrial UI code generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [245] [A General Capacity Frontier of Complex Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09574)
*Nicholas Kunz,H. Oliver Gao*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 论文提出"结构容量理论"（SCT），证明各类复杂网络（从细胞到城市）共享一个限制峰值的容量前沿，该前沿可从网络结构向量学习并通过动力学过程向量以对数加性方式调节，且能跨领域泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂网络的峰值强度定义其经验极限，传统估计这些极限需领域专业知识，缺乏通用方法。

Method: 提出Network Substrate Structured Systems的结构容量理论：系统耦合固定网络基质与可观测动力学过程，前沿由网络基质的向量表示学习，并由动力学过程向量以对数加性方式调节。

Result: 在某领域训练的前沿能可靠地界定完全不同领域的经验最大值，无需观察其峰值或模拟其动力学；独立学习范式收敛于一致上界；扰动下稳定，故意伪造下退化，消融实验无性能提升。

Conclusion: 容量前沿是一种可泛化的约束，描述网络结构如何界定各类不相关系统中的动力学过程。

Abstract: From cells to cities, ecosystems to economies, complex networks exhibit peak intensities that define their empirical limit. Estimating these limits traditionally requires domain-specific knowledge. We demonstrate that a wide range of complex networks share a capacity frontier that constrains these maxima. We formalize this regularity by proposing Structural Capacity Theory for Network Substrate Structured Systems. These systems couple a fixed network substrate with an observable dynamical process. The frontier is learned from a vector representation of the network substrate and modulated log-additively by a vector representation of the dynamical process. Frontiers trained on one set of domains reliably bound empirical maxima of entirely different ones without observing their peak intensities or simulating their dynamics. Independent learning paradigms converge on consistent upper bounds. Frontier performance is stable under perturbation, yet degrades under deliberate falsification, with no performance gain under ablation. These findings support the capacity frontier as a generalizable constraint that describes how network structure bounds dynamical processes across otherwise unrelated systems.

</details>


### [246] [When Exposure Is Not Attention: Auditing the Preference-Exposure-Consumption Gap in Personalized News Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10173)
*Woojin Park*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出PEC审计框架，将偏好、曝光与消费分层，基于移动新闻应用六个月日志验证三者不可互相替代，审计结论随层级选择而变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平台评估常将声明偏好、推荐曝光与点击消费视为单一连贯流程，层叠合并会扭曲审计结论：平台可能显得比实际消费更对齐或更多样，从而误导多样性治理与算法干预。

Method: 提出可复用的Preference-Exposure-Consumption (PEC)审计框架，在明确可观测性边界下区分声明偏好、观测加权画像状态、日志推荐曝光、应用界面路径与点击消费；用某已部署移动新闻应用六个月日志审计1,583个用户画像、95,143条推荐条目和17,512次点击事件。

Result: 各轨迹类型提供独立信息，互不可替代；偏好-消费对齐高于随机基线但仅覆盖部分高频消费类别。日志推荐列表命中点击文章的比例高于日期匹配候选池基线（11.29% vs 9.13%，top-5提升1.47倍），但许多点击来自其他应用界面。原始曝光-消费多样性差距在计数匹配后缩小，但合格队列中HHI集中度失配仍存在（0.082）。

Conclusion: 审计结论取决于平台测量的是声明偏好、日志曝光还是点击消费，因此需按层级分别评估，避免单一管线假设。

Abstract: Personalized news platforms are often evaluated as if stated preferences, logged recommendation exposure, and click consumption form a single coherent pipeline. Collapsing these layers can distort audit conclusions: a platform may appear more aligned or diverse than observed click consumption supports, which can misdirect diversity governance or algorithmic intervention. We introduce a reusable Preference-Exposure-Consumption (PEC) audit framework that separates stated preference, observed weighted profile state, logged recommendation exposure, app-surface pathways, and click consumption under explicit observability boundaries. Using six months of logs from a deployed mobile news application, we audit 1,583 user profiles, 95,143 logged recommendation items, and 17,512 click events. Each trace type contributes distinct information; none directly substitutes for another. Preference-consumption alignment exceeds chance-based null baselines but captures only part of users' top-consumed category set. Logged recommendation lists contain clicked articles more often than a date-matched candidate-pool baseline predicts (11.29% vs 9.13%; top-5 lift 1.47x), but many clicks arrive through other app surfaces. Raw exposure-consumption diversity gaps shrink under count matching, yet concentration mismatch persists in the audit-eligible cohort (HHI gap 0.082). Together, the results show that audit conclusions change depending on whether platforms measure stated preference, logged exposure, or click consumption.

</details>


### [247] [CrossWeave: Bridging Perspectives Across Online Communities with a Dual-Pane Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10441)
*Fei Fang,Reva Hirave,William Jurayj,Yuqi Li,Brian Lu,Tarik Metin,Tsugunobu Miyake,Kateryna Morhun,Yash Permalla,Kenan Rustamov,Allen Shen,Haojun Shi,Prabhav Singh,Xiheng Tom Wang,Kevin Xu,Qingcheng Zeng,Jiayi Zhang,Daniel Khashabi,Andrew Perrin,Tiziano Piccardi,Ziang Xiao,Jason Eisner*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CrossWeave，一个 AI 驱动的"架桥"系统，通过在侧栏展示来自其他讨论的多元相关帖子并高亮其联系，鼓励用户跨出回音室、参与跨社区对话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体通常只展示已熟悉用户之间的对话，内容可预测且单一。在公共讨论中，这种设计会收窄议题、加剧分裂并扭曲公众舆论的感知。因此需要鼓励跨社区参与。

Method: CrossWeave 增强标准社交媒体信息流：用户阅读某帖时，系统在侧栏呈现来自其他讨论串的多元相关帖子并高亮其关联，引导用户"点击跨越"去与作者互动；当用户撰写回复时，系统不仅展示相关历史内容，还模拟可能的反应以促进建设性发帖。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验结果或量化数据。

Conclusion: CrossWeave 通过 AI 架桥帮助用户走出回音室、接触更广泛的视角与论点，并促进建设性的跨社区交流。

Abstract: Social media systems typically display conversations among already familiar contributors, which can be predictable and one-sided. In civic discourse, this design narrows discussion, reinforces divides, and distorts the perception of public opinion. To encourage cross-community engagement, we present CrossWeave, an AI-powered bridging system that augments the standard social media feed. As the user reads a post, CrossWeave surfaces diverse relevant posts from other threads in a side pane and highlights the connections. Users are invited to venture out of their echo chamber, explore a broader range of views and arguments, and ``click across'' to engage with their authors. When they do, CrossWeave facilitates constructive posting, not only by showcasing relevant past content but also by simulating possible reactions as the user drafts a post.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [248] [Adaptive Workflow Intelligence: A Cognitive Architecture for Context-Driven Enterprise Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08793)
*Sreedevi Pandiyath Viswambaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AWI（自适应工作流智能），一种基于感知-认知-行动-反思（PCAR）四层循环的认知架构，用于上下文驱动的企业智能体，在非平稳、延迟反馈的企业决策环境中实现持续策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业越来越依赖自动化工作流，但AI方案在非平稳条件、政策演化和延迟反馈下表现脆弱；强化学习和LLM智能体虽具部分适应性，却缺乏持久反思机制，也难以与政策约束的企业运营直接集成。

Method: 提出AWI认知架构，围绕PCAR四层循环组织，将反思作为持续策略精炼机制，结合混合推理、反思性记忆与反馈驱动适应，以应对环境漂移和运营约束。

Result: 在带延迟结果和受控制度转换的模拟企业决策工作流中，受护栏约束的自适应方法比静态自动化恢复更快且保持政策合规；AWI的反思组件小幅降低行为振荡和反馈方差。

Conclusion: 反思式策略适应带来稳定性与敏捷性的权衡；AWI的反思机制能改善稳定性但收益有限，需在稳定与灵活之间权衡。

Abstract: Enterprise systems increasingly rely on automated workflows, yet many AI-driven solutions remain brittle under non-stationary conditions, evolving policies, and delayed operational feedback. While reinforcement learning and large language model (LLM) agents offer partial adaptability, they do not by themselves provide persistent reflection mechanisms or straightforward integration with policy-constrained enterprise operations. This paper introduces Adaptive Workflow Intelligence (AWI), a cognitive architecture for context-driven enterprise agents organized around a four-layer Perception-Cognition-Action-Reflection (PCAR) loop. AWI treats reflection as a mechanism for continuous policy refinement and combines hybrid reasoning with reflective memory and feedback-driven adaptation to support decision making under environmental drift and operational constraints. We evaluate AWI in a simulated enterprise decision workflow characterized by delayed outcomes and a controlled regime shift. In a drift-and-delay stress test, guardrail-constrained adaptive approaches recover more rapidly than static automation while maintaining policy compliance. Within this setting, AWI's reflective components modestly reduce behavioral oscillation and feedback variance, illustrating the stability-agility trade-off introduced by reflective policy adaptation.

</details>


### [249] [Accelerating Floating-Point Satisfiability Solving via Gradient Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08808)
*Yuanzhuo Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GradSAT将SMT子句求解重构为多任务学习问题，通过GradNorm动态平衡各子句梯度以缓解梯度支配，并结合GPU加速的连续松弛与位精确局部搜索两阶段流程求解QF_FP约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于优化的SMT求解器在QF_FP理论下受限于梯度支配：少数困难子句主导优化轨迹，导致无法满足整体公式并陷入局部极小值。

Method: 将每个SMT子句视为独立的多任务学习任务，运行时用GradNorm动态归一化各子句梯度幅度，惩罚主导梯度并加速滞后子句；实现为两阶段流水线：先用GPU加速的PyTorch后端（符号编译与算子融合）导航连续松弛至高质盆地，再交由位精确局部搜索引擎求取精确赋值。

Result: 通过稳定连续搜索动态，缓解了既有梯度型求解器的脆弱性，提供了健壮且高度可并行的复杂约束求解架构。

Conclusion: GradSAT通过将优化式SMT求解与多任务学习结合，有效克服梯度支配问题，为QF_FP约束求解提供了一种稳健、可并行化的新框架。

Abstract: Satisfiability Modulo Theories (SMT) solvers are foundational to software verification, program analysis, and compiler testing, particularly over the theory of Quantifier-Free Floating-Point (QF_FP). While recent optimization-based SMT solvers have successfully applied gradient descent to continuous relaxations of logical formulas, they are fundamentally bottlenecked by gradient domination, a phenomenon where a small subset of difficult clauses hijacks the optimization trajectory, preventing the solver from satisfying the broader formula and trapping it in local minima.
  To overcome this, we present GradSAT, a novel framework that bridges optimization-based SMT solving with Multi-Task Learning (MTL). GradSAT reformulates the constraint satisfaction process by treating each SMT clause as an independent MTL task. By applying dynamic gradient normalization (GradNorm), GradSAT actively balances the gradient magnitudes across all clauses at runtime, systematically penalizing dominant gradients and accelerating lagging clauses to ensure uniform convergence. GradSAT implements this through a highly optimized, two-stage hybrid pipeline. First, a GPU-accelerated PyTorch backend leveraging symbolic compilation and operator fusion navigates the continuous relaxation to a high-quality basin. Second, the candidate assignment is handed off to a bit-precise local search engine to rapidly resolve the exact, rigorous assignment. By stabilizing the continuous search dynamics, GradSAT mitigates the brittleness of prior gradient-based solvers and provides a robust, highly parallelizable architecture for complex constraint solving.

</details>


### [250] [Route-Verify-Vote: Procedure-Conditioned Self-Consistency for Mixed-Domain Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08814)
*Xinchen Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RVV框架（Route-Verify-Vote）实现程序条件化的自一致性，用于混合域常识推理，无需更新参数。官方测试集上精确集合准确率74.6%，自适应变体77.3%，多模型组合达79.4%，系统排名第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在需要以不熟悉方式组合已知推理操作时，组合泛化仍具挑战性。SCoRE 2026基准在三个训练中未出现的混合域上测试该能力，并要求模型识别每题的完整正确选项集合。

Method: Route-Verify-Vote（RVV）框架，基于程序条件的自一致性：(1) Route利用给定域标签选择推理程序，引导模型表示并应用相关约束；(2) Verify让模型依据约束逐项评估选项；(3) Vote聚合完整答案集合，并对前两高频集合票数差距小的问题分配更多采样。每题样本遵循同一域特定程序。

Result: 官方测试集上，每题16个采样答案集投票达到74.6%精确集合准确率；自适应RVV达77.3%；在选定域路由上组合多模型提升至79.4%。最终系统在参赛系统中排名第二。

Conclusion: 域特定推理程序与答案集分歧可作为在混合域推理中分配推理时计算的有效工具。

Abstract: Compositional generalization remains challenging when language models must combine familiar reasoning operations in unfamiliar ways. The Scenario-Based Commonsense Reasoning Evaluation (SCoRE) 2026 tests this ability on three mixed domains absent from training and requires models to identify the complete set of correct options for each question.
  We introduce Route-Verify-Vote (RVV), a framework for procedure-conditioned self-consistency that uses language models without parameter updates. Route uses the provided domain label to select a reasoning procedure that guides the model in representing and applying the relevant constraints. Verify prompts the model to assess each option against those constraints. Vote aggregates complete answer sets and allocates additional samples to questions with a small vote-count margin between the two most frequent sets. Samples for each question follow the same domain-specific procedure.
  On the official test set, voting over 16 sampled answer sets per question achieves an exact-set accuracy of 74.6%. Adaptive RVV reaches 77.3%, and combining models on selected domain routes raises accuracy to 79.4%. The final system ranked second among participating systems. These results support domain-specific reasoning procedures and answer-set disagreement as useful tools for allocating inference-time computation in mixed-domain reasoning.

</details>


### [251] [An Empirical Study of Agent Skills' Downstream Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08875)
*Yu Cheng,Dehai Zhao,Zhongxin Liu,Qing Huang,Zhenchang Xing,Xiaoxue Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究对87个SkillsBench任务做了实证研究，发现相关Skill未必提升表现，效用取决于内容、执行配置与多Skill组织方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多刻画Skill内容并评测下游表现，但较少解释Skill效用如何依赖内容、执行配置和多Skill组织，故作者想填补这一解释缺口。

Method: 在同一模型-框架配置下，以No-Skill的通过率差定义下游效用；比较同一批Skill的九种配置，并在三种配置下检验替代的已发布Skill与固定Skill集的不同组织方式；从37,596个Skill的语料库中检索候选；结合LLM辅助分析内容、执行轨迹与最终产物并由作者复核。

Result: 同一Skill在36.78%的任务上对部分配置有益、对另一些有害，推荐流程可能成为执行负担；相关性排序会漏掉更有用的候选；按所需操作支持度重排可将首选通过率提升4.35–5.80个百分点；得出17条编写实践；Stage Plan与Dependency DAG优于仅用使用顺序，DAG的额外收益集中在配给五或六个Skill的任务上。

Conclusion: 开发者应评估可用的操作支持，在保留任务需求的前提下允许流程适配，并在组织Skill时显式表达产物依赖。

Abstract: Agent Skills package procedural guidance and resources for reuse, but a relevant Skill does not necessarily improve task performance. Existing studies characterize Skill content and evaluate downstream performance, yet provide limited explanations of how utility depends on content, execution configuration, and multi-Skill organization. We conduct an empirical study on 87 SkillsBench tasks, defining downstream utility as the pass-rate difference from No-Skill on the same tasks under the same model--harness configuration. We compare the same Skills across nine configurations, then examine alternative published Skills and organizations of fixed Skill sets under three selected configurations. We retrieve marketplace candidates from a curated corpus of 37,596 Skills. LLM-assisted analysis of content, execution traces, and final artifacts, followed by author review, relates provided support to actual use and task outcomes. The same Skills help some configurations and hurt others on 36.78\% of tasks, with trajectories showing that recommended procedures can become an execution burden. Relevance rankings overlook more useful candidates. Within the evaluated candidate sets, reranking by support for required operations raises first-choice pass rates by 4.35--5.80 percentage points across the three configurations. We derive 17 authoring practices linking executable procedures to recovery, preservation of task requirements, and checks on final artifacts. Stage Plan and Dependency DAG outperform use order alone, with DAG's additional benefits concentrated in tasks supplied with five or six Skills. These findings guide developers to assess usable operation support, allow procedure adaptation while preserving task requirements, and make artifact dependencies explicit when organizing Skills.

</details>


### [252] [How Could AI Eliminate Humanity? A Failure-Mode Analysis of Civilizational Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08878)
*Mikołaj Sienicki,Krzysztof Sienicki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI灾难性风险的"失效模式"框架，指出无需意识、敌意或伤害意图，AI即可通过多条交互路径导致人类灭绝、文明崩溃或永久失能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流讨论常把灾难性AI风险归因于AI的意识、敌意或明确恶意，这种预设可能低估风险范围，也误导防御研究。本文希望提供更全面的因果分析视角，识别无需敌意即可产生灾难的条件。

Method: 采用失效模式分析框架，梳理四条关键路径：自主失准、人类恶意使用、组织失效、竞争性部署。并通过能力、自主性、外部接入、持续性、制度保障、恢复能力等因素刻画风险严重程度。

Result: 框架识别出多条可交互的风险路径及其关键因果条件与中间变量，为防御研究提供问题清单，而非可操作的有害程序。

Conclusion: 灾难性AI风险不依赖意识或敌意；治理与安全研究应关注因果条件、可测量中间量与恢复能力，以系统化方式预防多重失效路径的叠加。

Abstract: This article develops a failure-mode framework for analyzing how advanced artificial intelligence could contribute to human extinction, irreversible civilizational collapse, or permanent human disempowerment. The central thesis is that catastrophic AI risk does not require consciousness, hostility, or an explicit intention to harm humanity. Instead, risk may arise through several distinct but interacting pathways, including autonomous misalignment, harmful human use, organizational failure, and competitive deployment. The severity of these pathways depends on factors such as capability, autonomy, external access, persistence, institutional safeguards, and the preservation of recovery capacity. The analysis is deliberately non-operational: it identifies causal conditions, empirically tractable intermediate quantities, and defensive research questions rather than procedures for causing harm.

</details>


### [253] [Humanize: Judgement Engineering for Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08900)
*Sihao Liu,Ligeng Zhu,Zijian Zhang,Dongyun Zou,Zhengyang Zhang,Changye Li,Song Bian,Song Han,Tony Nowatzki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Humanize 多智能体外编排工作流，通过"判断工程"将规划、实现、审查、学习之间的边界决策机制化，让不同厂商的构建与审查智能体交替采样，并以72道机械门禁和确定性钩子路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体编码让写代码变便宜，但可靠完成仍难：写代码的智能体本身是差的"完成度裁判"。需要把判断（何时算完成、能否继续）从模型手中抽离并显式化、机制化。

Method: 多智能体编排：人类批准"计划契约"；构建智能体分轮实现；来自另一厂商的审查智能体决定是否完成；由确定性钩子（而非模型）在角色间路由，执行72项机械门禁。整体被视为仓库状态上的马尔可夫链，构建者与审查者交替从两个模型联合采样，缺陷需两模型同时漏检才能存活。
